	<script>
		(function () {
			var BLOCKED_SIZES = { '1280x1200': true, '1366x1366': true };
			var MARFEEL_PATTERN = /mrf\.io|marfeel/i;

			function getScreenSize() {
				if (typeof screen === 'undefined' || !screen.width || !screen.height) {
					return null;
				}

				return screen.width + 'x' + screen.height;
			}

			function setCookie(name, value) {
				var secure = location.protocol === 'https:' ? '; Secure' : '';
				document.cookie = name + '=' + encodeURIComponent(value)
					+ '; path=/'
					+ '; max-age=86400'
					+ '; SameSite=Lax'
					+ secure;
			}

			function isMarfeelScript(node) {
				if (!node || node.tagName !== 'SCRIPT') {
					return false;
				}

				var src = node.src || node.getAttribute('src') || '';
				if (MARFEEL_PATTERN.test(src)) {
					return true;
				}

				var text = node.text || node.textContent || '';
				return MARFEEL_PATTERN.test(text);
			}

			function blockMarfeelScripts() {
				window.bnBlockMarfeel = true;

				var originalCreateElement = document.createElement.bind(document);
				document.createElement = function (tagName) {
					var el = originalCreateElement(tagName);
					if (String(tagName).toLowerCase() !== 'script') {
						return el;
					}

					var blocked = false;
					Object.defineProperty(el, 'src', {
						configurable: true,
						enumerable: true,
						set: function (value) {
							if (!blocked && typeof value === 'string' && MARFEEL_PATTERN.test(value)) {
								blocked = true;
								return;
							}
							el.setAttribute('src', value);
						},
						get: function () {
							return el.getAttribute('src') || '';
						}
					});

					return el;
				};

				if (typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') {
					return;
				}

				new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
					mutations.forEach(function (mutation) {
						mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function (node) {
							if (!isMarfeelScript(node)) {
								return;
							}
							if (node.parentNode) {
								node.parentNode.removeChild(node);
							}
						});
					});
				}).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
			}

			function blockPage() {
				blockMarfeelScripts();

				if (document.readyState === 'loading' && typeof window.stop === 'function') {
					window.stop();
				}

				function hidePage() {
					if (!document.body) {
						return;
					}
					document.body.innerHTML = '';
					document.body.style.display = 'none';
				}

				if (document.body) {
					hidePage();
				} else {
					document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', hidePage, { once: true });
				}
			}

			var size = getScreenSize();
			if (!size) {
				return;
			}

			var match = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|; )bn_scr=([^;]*)/);
			var current = match ? decodeURIComponent(match[1]) : null;
			if (current !== size) {
				setCookie('bn_scr', size);
			}

			if (BLOCKED_SIZES[size]) {
				blockPage();
			}
		})();
	</script>

	<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
		$(document).ready(function(){
            // Modals are using jquery.modal for now, and not bootstrap
            if (typeof bootstrap !== 'undefined' && bootstrap.Modal) {
                $.fn.bootstrapModal = function (action) {
                    return this.each(function () {
                        if (action === 'show') {
                            bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(this).show();
                        } else if (action === 'hide') {
                            var instance = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(this);
                            if (instance) {
                                instance.hide();
                            }
                        } else if (action === 'toggle') {
                            bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(this).toggle();
                        } else if (action === 'dispose') {
                            var modalInstance = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(this);
                            if (modalInstance) {
                                modalInstance.dispose();
                            }
                        }
                    });
                };
            } else if (typeof $.fn.modal.noConflict === 'function') {
                var bootstrapModal = $.fn.modal.noConflict() // return $.fn.modal to previously assigned value
                $.fn.bootstrapModal = bootstrapModal // give $().bootstrapModal the Bootstrap functionality
            }

			// Modal
            
			var modal = $("#mainSiteModal");
			var modal_show_speed = 200;

			function closeMainSiteModal() {
				$(document).off('click.mainSiteModalBackdrop');
				modal.find(".modal-content").css('width', '');
				modal.find(".modal-html").html('');
				modal.hide();
				modal.removeClass('is-visible');
			}

			function bindMainSiteModalBackdrop() {
				$(document).off('click.mainSiteModalBackdrop');
				setTimeout(function () {
					if (!modal.hasClass('is-visible')) {
						return;
					}
					$(document).on('click.mainSiteModalBackdrop', function () {
						closeMainSiteModal();
					});
				}, 0);
			}

			$(".mainSiteModalButton").click(function() {
                var html_link = $(this).attr('href');
                var preloaded_content_holder = $(this).data('content-holder');

				if ($(this).attr('data-modal-size')) {
					switch ($(this).attr('data-modal-size')) {
						case '50%':
							modal.find(".modal-content").addClass('modal-content-50');
							break;
					}
				}

				modal.fadeIn(modal_show_speed);
				modal.addClass('is-visible');
                if (!preloaded_content_holder) {
                    $.get(html_link, {ajax: true}, function (data) {
                        modal.find(".modal-html").html(data);
                    });
                } else {
                    modal.find(".modal-html").html($(preloaded_content_holder).html());
                    if (preloaded_content_holder === '#login_form') {
                        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('bn:auth-modal-open'));
                    }
                }
				bindMainSiteModalBackdrop();
				return false;
			});
			$('#mainSiteModal .modal-content').click(function(event){
				event.stopPropagation();
			});
			$("#mainSiteModalClose").click(function(event) {
				closeMainSiteModal();
			});

            // Form submit by ajax
            $(document).on('submit', "form.submitAjax", function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

				const url = "/index.php";
                const formObj = $(this);
				const submitButton = formObj.find('button[type="submit"]');

				submitButton.prop('disabled', true);
				formObj.addClass('loading');

				$.ajax({
					type: "POST",
					url: url,
					data: formObj.serialize(),
					dataType: 'json',
					success: function(data)
					{
                        if (data) {
                            if (data.auth_exchange_url || data.redirect) {
                                try {
                                    if (window.marfeel && Array.isArray(window.marfeel.cmd)) {
                                        window.marfeel.cmd.push(['compass', function (compass) {
                                            compass.setSiteUserId(data.user_id);
                                            compass.setUserType(data.user_level > 0 ? '3' : '2');
                                        }]);
                                    }
    								registerAuthEvent('login');
                                } catch (e) {
                                    console.error(e);
                                }

                                handleAuthLoginRedirect(data, formObj.find('input[name=ref_url]').val());
                            }

                            if (data.messages && data.messages.error) {
								errorContainer.show(data.messages.error);
							}

							submitButton.prop('disabled', false);
							formObj.removeClass('loading');
                        }
					}
				});

				return false;
			});

            // Remove unneeded _r param after reload
            if (location.search.includes('_r=')) {
                const currentUrl = new URL(location);
                currentUrl.searchParams.delete('_r');
                history.replaceState(null, '', currentUrl);
            }
		});

        const errorContainer = {
            show: (errorMessage) => {
                const legacyErrorContainer = $('form.submitAjax').find('.form_error_message');
                const errorContainer = $('form.submitAjax').find('.form-error-message');
				legacyErrorContainer.add(errorContainer).hide();

                if (errorContainer.closest('.design-v2').length > 0) {
                    errorContainer.html(`<div class="alert alert-danger fw-normal rounded-1 mb-6" role="alert">${errorMessage}</div>`);
                    errorContainer.slideDown(200);
                } else {
                    legacyErrorContainer.html(errorMessage); // Legacy fallback
                    legacyErrorContainer.show();
                }
            },
            hide: () => {
                $('form.submitAjax').find('.form-error-message').hide();
                $('form.submitAjax').find('.form_error_message').hide();
            }
        }

        function locationReload(url = '') {
            let target = url ? new URL(url) : new URL(location.href);

            // Only add _r param if the URL is the same as current (otherwise ads can prevent reload)
            if (target.href === location.href) {
                target.searchParams.set('_r', Date.now().toString(36));
            }

            window.location.replace(target.href);
        }

        function handleAuthLoginRedirect(data, fallbackUrl) {
            if (fallbackUrl && fallbackUrl.indexOf('#') !== -1) {
                fallbackUrl = fallbackUrl.split('#')[0];
            }

            if (!window.ReactNativeWebView) {
                if (data && data.auth_exchange_url) {
                    window.location.replace(data.auth_exchange_url);
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (fallbackUrl) {
                window.location.replace(fallbackUrl);
                return;
            }

            if (data && data.redirect) {
                let redirectUrl = data.redirect;
                if (!/^[a-z][a-z\d+\-.]*:\/\//i.test(redirectUrl)) {
                    redirectUrl = new URL(redirectUrl, location.href).href;
                }
                locationReload(redirectUrl.split('#')[0]);
                return;
            }

            locationReload(window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
        }
        

		const analyticsEnabled = true;
		function registerAuthEvent(eventAction) {
			registerAnalyticsEvent('NTG account', eventAction, 'success');
		}

		function registerAnalyticsEvent(category, action, label) {
            try {
    			if (analyticsEnabled && typeof ga === 'function') {
    				ga('send', 'event', {
    					eventCategory: category,
    					eventAction: action,
    					eventLabel: label,
    					nonInteraction: false
    				});
    			}
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
            }
		}

		function registerScrollDepthEvent(action, label, value) {
            try {
    			if (analyticsEnabled && typeof ga === 'function') {
    				ga('send', {
    					hitType: 'event',
    					eventCategory: 'NTG article milestone',
    					eventAction: action,
    					eventLabel: label,
    					eventValue: value,
    					nonInteraction: true
    				});
    			}
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
            }
		}
	</script>

                                <script>
                function loadAds() {
    var firstAd = null;
    window.googletag_ads = true;
    let lgSize = window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--bs-breakpoint-lg');
    let xlSize = window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--bs-breakpoint-xl');

    let adsConfig = [];

    window.googletag.cmd.push(() => {
        function defineAdSlot(adUnitId, containerId, sizes) {
            if (window.firstAd === null) {
                window.firstAd = containerId;
            }
            const slot = window.googletag.defineSlot(adUnitId, sizes, containerId);
            slot.addService(window.googletag.pubads());
            return slot;
        }

        let sizeToMatch = window.matchMedia("(min-width: "+ xlSize +")").matches ? ['xl', 'desktop'] : 
            (window.matchMedia("(min-width: "+ lgSize +")").matches ? ['lg', 'desktop'] : ['mobile']);

        let adTypeMap = {};
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-type]').forEach(div => {
            if (!div.hasAttribute('data-ad-defined')) {
                adTypeMap[div.id] = div.getAttribute('data-ad-type');
            }
        });

        Object.entries(adTypeMap).forEach(([id, type]) => {
            if (adsConfig[type] && sizeToMatch.includes(adsConfig[type].screen_type)) {
                defineAdSlot(adsConfig[type].ad_type, id, adsConfig[type].size);
                document.getElementById(id).setAttribute('data-ad-defined', 'true');
            }
        });

        window.googletag.setConfig({
            lazyLoad: {
                renderMarginPercent: 50,
                fetchMarginPercent: 200
            }
        });

        // Register event handlers to observe lazy loading behavior.
        window.googletag.pubads().addEventListener("slotRequested", (event) => {
            console.log(event.slot.getSlotElementId(), "fetched");
        });

        window.googletag.pubads().addEventListener("slotOnload", (event) => {
            console.log(event.slot.getSlotElementId(), "rendered");
        });

        var firstTextContainer = document.querySelector('.text_container[data-id]');
        if (firstTextContainer) {
            var articleId = firstTextContainer.getAttribute('data-id');
            if (articleId) {
                window.googletag.setConfig({
                    targeting: {
                        article_id: articleId
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        window.googletag.setConfig({
            targeting: {
                page_version: 'us'
            }
        });

        // Enable SRA and services.
        window.googletag.setConfig({
            singleRequest: true
        });
        window.googletag.enableServices();
    });
}            </script>
            

									<script>
		(function () {
			var prefetchByEagerness = {
				moderate: [
					'/',
					'/news.html',
					'/games.html',
					'/video/all/podcasts.html'
				],
				eager: []
			};

			var additionalPrefetchUrls = [{"url":"\/news-257182-christian-koloko-baskonia-offer-euroleague-nba.html","eagerness":"eager"},{"url":"\/news-257223-lebron-james-issues-warning-to-nba-about-lakers-rival-and-title-contender.html","eagerness":"moderate"},{"url":"\/news-257222-dangelo-russell-makes-big-statement-after-sensational-china-league-debut.html","eagerness":"moderate"}];

			additionalPrefetchUrls.forEach(function (item) {
				if (!item || !item.url) {
					return;
				}

				var eagerness = item.eagerness === 'eager' ? 'eager' : 'moderate';
				if (prefetchByEagerness[eagerness].indexOf(item.url) === -1) {
					prefetchByEagerness[eagerness].push(item.url);
				}
			});

			var allPrefetchUrls = prefetchByEagerness.eager.concat(prefetchByEagerness.moderate);

			function addPrefetchLink(href) {
				if (document.querySelector('link[rel="prefetch"][href="' + href + '"]')) {
					return;
				}

				var linkElem = document.createElement('link');
				linkElem.rel = 'prefetch';
				linkElem.href = href;
				document.head.appendChild(linkElem);
			}

			if (HTMLScriptElement.supports && HTMLScriptElement.supports('speculationrules')) {
				var prefetchRules = ['eager', 'moderate'].reduce(function (rules, eagerness) {
					if (prefetchByEagerness[eagerness].length) {
						rules.push({
							source: 'list',
							urls: prefetchByEagerness[eagerness],
							eagerness: eagerness
						});
					}

					return rules;
				}, []);

				var specScript = document.createElement('script');
				specScript.type = 'speculationrules';
				specScript.textContent = JSON.stringify({ prefetch: prefetchRules });
				document.head.appendChild(specScript);
			} else {
				prefetchByEagerness.eager.forEach(addPrefetchLink);

				document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (event) {
					var anchor = event.target.closest('a[href]');
					if (!anchor) {
						return;
					}

					var href;
					try {
						href = new URL(anchor.getAttribute('href'), location.origin).pathname;
					} catch (e) {
						return;
					}

					if (allPrefetchUrls.indexOf(href) === -1) {
						return;
					}

					addPrefetchLink(href);
				}, true);
			}
		})();
		</script>

<script>
(function() {
    function isAndroidApp() {
        return !!(navigator.userAgent && navigator.userAgent.indexOf('android_app') !== -1);
    }

    if (!isAndroidApp()) {
        return;
    }

    window.BN_APP_HIDE_PAYMENTS = true;
    document.documentElement.classList.add('app-hide-payments');
    document.body.classList.add('app-hide-payments');

    function isPliusPagePath(pathname) {
        var path = (pathname || '').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
        return path === '/plius' || path === '/plus';
    }

    function isPaymentUrl(href) {
        if (!href) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            var url = new URL(href, window.location.origin);

            if (isPliusPagePath(url.pathname)) {
                return true;
            }

            var moduleName = url.searchParams.get('module');
            var actionName = url.searchParams.get('action');

            if (moduleName === 'memberships') {
                return true;
            }

            if (moduleName === 'orders' && (actionName === 'purchase' || actionName === 'subscribe')) {
                return true;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            if (/module=memberships|module=orders[^&]*&action=(purchase|subscribe)/i.test(href)) {
                return true;
            }

            return /(?:^|\/)(plius|plus)(?:\/|$|\?|#)/i.test(href);
        }

        return false;
    }

    if (isPliusPagePath(window.location.pathname) || isPaymentUrl(window.location.href)) {
        window.location.replace('/');
        return;
    }

    function hidePaymentElements() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.bn-plus-bar, [class*="bn-plus-bar"]').forEach(function(el) {
            el.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    function hidePaymentLinks() {
        document.querySelectorAll('a[href]').forEach(function(link) {
            if (!isPaymentUrl(link.getAttribute('href'))) {
                return;
            }

            var item = link.closest('li') || link.closest('.mobile-plus-subscribe') || link;
            item.style.display = 'none';
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
        var link = event.target.closest('a[href]');
        if (!link) {
            return;
        }

        if (link.classList.contains('bn-plus-bar') || [].some.call(link.classList, function(className) {
            return className.indexOf('bn-plus-bar') === 0;
        })) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            return;
        }

        if (!isPaymentUrl(link.getAttribute('href'))) {
            return;
        }

        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
    }, true);

    hidePaymentElements();
    hidePaymentLinks();

    if (typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined') {
        new MutationObserver(function() {
            hidePaymentElements();
            hidePaymentLinks();
        }).observe(document.documentElement, {
            childList: true,
            subtree: true
        });
    }
})();
</script>

    <script>
window.bnSearchEngineCode = 'bb9b7b00ca5e6df87';
window.bnFacebookSdkLocale = 'en_US';

(function () {
    window.bnWhenIdle = window.bnWhenIdle || function (fn) {
        var run = function () {
            try { fn(); } catch (e) {}
        };
        var schedule = function () {
            if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
                requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 4000 });
            } else {
                setTimeout(run, 1);
            }
        };
        if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
            schedule();
        } else {
            window.addEventListener('load', schedule);
        }
    };

    function bnWaitFor(test, done, maxTries) {
        var tries = 0;
        maxTries = maxTries || 60;
        (function tick() {
            var value;
            try {
                value = test();
            } catch (e) {
                value = null;
            }
            if (value || tries++ >= maxTries) {
                done(value || null);
                return;
            }
            setTimeout(tick, 50);
        })();
    }

    function bnCseApiReady() {
        return !!(window.google
            && window.google.search
            && window.google.search.cse
            && window.google.search.cse.element
            && typeof window.google.search.cse.element.getElement === 'function');
    }

    window.bnLoadGoogleCse = function (done) {
        if (typeof done === 'function') {
            (window.bnGoogleCsePending = window.bnGoogleCsePending || []).push(done);
        }

        function flush() {
            if (window.bnGoogleCseLoaded) {
                var extra = window.bnGoogleCsePending || [];
                window.bnGoogleCsePending = [];
                for (var j = 0; j < extra.length; j++) {
                    try { extra[j](); } catch (e) {}
                }
                return;
            }
            window.bnGoogleCseLoaded = true;
            var queue = window.bnGoogleCsePending || [];
            window.bnGoogleCsePending = [];
            for (var i = 0; i < queue.length; i++) {
                try { queue[i](); } catch (e) {}
            }
        }

        function markReady() {
            bnWaitFor(bnCseApiReady, function (ready) {
                if (ready) {
                    flush();
                }
            });
        }

        if (window.bnGoogleCseLoaded) {
            flush();
            return;
        }
        if (window.bnGoogleCseLoading) {
            return;
        }

        var cx = window.bnSearchEngineCode;
        if (!cx) {
            return;
        }

        window.bnGoogleCseLoading = true;
        var prev = (window.__gcse && window.__gcse.callback) || null;
        window.__gcse = window.__gcse || {};
        window.__gcse.callback = function () {
            if (typeof prev === 'function') {
                prev();
            }
            markReady();
        };

        var script = document.createElement('script');
        script.async = true;
        script.src = 'https://cse.google.com/cse.js?cx=' + encodeURIComponent(cx);
        script.onload = function () {
            markReady();
        };
        script.onerror = function () {
            window.bnGoogleCseLoading = false;
        };
        (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
    };

    window.bnWhenGoogleCseElement = function (gname, done) {
        window.bnLoadGoogleCse(function () {
            bnWaitFor(function () {
                return window.google.search.cse.element.getElement(gname);
            }, done);
        });
    };

    window.bnRunGoogleCseSearch = function (query) {
        if (window.jQuery) {
            window.jQuery('#search-modal').modal();
        }
        window.bnWhenGoogleCseElement('desktop_search', function (element) {
            if (element && query) {
                element.execute(query);
                return;
            }
            var input = document.querySelector('#search-modal .gsc-input input, #gsc-i-id1, .gsc-input input');
            if (input) {
                input.value = query || '';
                input.focus();
            }
            var button = document.querySelector('#search-modal .gsc-search-button-v2, #search-modal .gsc-search-button, .gsc-search-button-v2, .gsc-search-button');
            if (button && query) {
                button.click();
            }
        });
    };

    function isSearchUiTarget(node) {
        if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) {
            return false;
        }
        return !!(node.closest && node.closest(
            '#search-modal-button, [href="#search-modal"], #mobile-search-button, #navbar-search-container, #navbar-search-container-input, #search-input, #offcanvas-alt-search-form, #offcanvas-alt-search-input, .nav-link-mobile-search'
        ));
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        if (isSearchUiTarget(event.target)) {
            window.bnLoadGoogleCse();
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener('focusin', function (event) {
        if (isSearchUiTarget(event.target)) {
            window.bnLoadGoogleCse();
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
        if (isSearchUiTarget(event.target)) {
            window.bnLoadGoogleCse();
        }
    }, true);

    function parseFacebookWidgets() {
        if (window.FB && window.FB.XFBML) {
            window.FB.XFBML.parse();
        }
    }

    function loadFacebookSdk() {
        if (window.FB) {
            parseFacebookWidgets();
            return;
        }

        var prevInit = window.fbAsyncInit;
        window.fbAsyncInit = function () {
            if (typeof prevInit === 'function') {
                prevInit();
            } else if (window.FB && !window.bnFacebookSdkInited) {
                window.FB.init({
                    xfbml: true,
                    version: 'v2.5'
                });
            }
            window.bnFacebookSdkInited = true;
            parseFacebookWidgets();
        };

        if (document.getElementById('facebook-jssdk')) {
            return;
        }

        var script = document.createElement('script');
        script.id = 'facebook-jssdk';
        script.async = true;
        script.src = '//connect.facebook.net/' + (window.bnFacebookSdkLocale || 'en_US') + '/sdk.js#xfbml=1&version=v2.5';
        (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
    }

    function observeFacebookWidgets() {
        var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('.fb-comments, .fb-post, .fb-video');
        if (!nodes.length) {
            return;
        }
        if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
            loadFacebookSdk();
            return;
        }
        var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
            for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
                if (entries[i].isIntersecting) {
                    observer.disconnect();
                    loadFacebookSdk();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }, { rootMargin: '400px 0px' });
        for (var j = 0; j < nodes.length; j++) {
            observer.observe(nodes[j]);
        }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', observeFacebookWidgets);
    } else {
        observeFacebookWidgets();
    }
})();

    let registerMarfeelUserType = function(data) {

            if (window.marfeel && Array.isArray(window.marfeel.cmd)) {
                window.marfeel.cmd.push(['compass', function (compass) {
                    compass.setSiteUserId(data.user_id);
                    compass.setUserType(data.user_level > 0 ? '3' : '2');
                }]);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(e);
        }
    }

	let fbRefUrl;
    // This is called with the results from from FB.getLoginStatus().
    function statusChangeCallback(response) {
        if (response.status === 'connected') {
        	//we still need additional permissions
			FB.login(function (response) {
				$.post("/?module=users&action=facebookLogin&ajax=1", { token : response.authResponse.accessToken}, function(data){
					if (data.success === true) {
						registerAuthEvent('login');
                        registerMarfeelUserType(data);
						handleAuthLoginRedirect(data, fbRefUrl);
					} else {
						if (data.messages && data.messages.error) {
							errorContainer.show(data.messages.error);
						}
					}
				});
			}, {scope: 'email,public_profile'});
        } else {
            FB.login(function(response) {
                if (response.authResponse) {
                    $.post("/?module=users&action=facebookLogin&ajax=1", { token : response.authResponse.accessToken}, function(data){
                        registerMarfeelUserType(data);
						if (data.success === true) {
							registerAuthEvent('login');
							handleAuthLoginRedirect(data, fbRefUrl);
						} else {
							if (data.messages && data.messages.error) {
								errorContainer.show(data.messages.error);
							}
						}
                    });
                }
            }, {scope: 'email,public_profile'});
        }
    }

    // This function is called when someone finishes with the Login

    function loginFb(element) {
        if (window.ReactNativeWebView) {
            window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify({ action: 'login', data: { type: 'facebook' } }));
            return;
        } else {
            fbRefUrl = $(element).closest("form").find("input[name='ref_url']").val();
            errorContainer.hide();
            FB.getLoginStatus(function(response) {
                statusChangeCallback(response);
            });
        }
    }

<script>
(function () {
    if (window.bnGoogleAuth) {
        return;
    }

    var clientId = '234850334545-125sfg6ek4ait7j50m14g2t7j6kscqs7.apps.googleusercontent.com';
    var mode = 'login';
    var connectDisplay = '';
    var disconnectConfirm = 'Are you sure want to disconnect your Google account?';
    // Hardcoded: make_url has no pretty-URL map entry for googleStart and becomes "".
    var googleStartPath = '/index.php?module=users&action=googleStart';

    function stripUrlHash(url) {
        if (!url) {
            return url;
        }
        var hashPos = url.indexOf('#');
        return hashPos === -1 ? url : url.substring(0, hashPos);
    }

    function currentCleanPath() {
        var path = window.location.pathname || '/';
        var search = window.location.search || '';
        if (search) {
            try {
                var params = new URLSearchParams(search);
                params.delete('ref_url');
                params.delete('code');
                params.delete('state');
                params.delete('error');
                var cleaned = params.toString();
                search = cleaned ? ('?' + cleaned) : '';
            } catch (e) {
                search = '';
            }
        }
        return path + search;
    }

    function normalizeRefUrl(refUrl) {
        refUrl = stripUrlHash(refUrl || '');
        if (!refUrl) {
            return '/';
        }
        if (refUrl.indexOf('ref_url=') !== -1) {
            try {
                var parsed = new URL(refUrl, window.location.origin);
                return parsed.pathname || '/';
            } catch (e) {
                return '/';
            }
        }
        return refUrl;
    }

    function clearGoogleOAuthHash() {
        var hash = window.location.hash || '';
        if (hash.indexOf('id_token=') === -1 && hash.indexOf('iss=') === -1) {
            return;
        }
        if (window.history && window.history.replaceState) {
            window.history.replaceState(null, '', currentCleanPath());
        }
    }

    function setButtonVisible($el, visible) {
        if (!$el.length) {
            return;
        }
        if (connectDisplay) {
            $el.css('display', visible ? connectDisplay : 'none');
        } else if (visible) {
            $el.show();
        } else {
            $el.hide();
        }
    }

    function setGoogleLinkState(connected) {
        setButtonVisible($('#connect-google'), !connected);
        setButtonVisible($('#disconnect-google'), connected);
        if (connected) {
            $('#google-status-connected').show();
            $('#google-status-disconnected').hide();
        } else {
            $('#google-status-connected').hide();
            $('#google-status-disconnected').show();
        }
    }

    function hideLoginError() {
        if (typeof errorContainer !== 'undefined' && errorContainer.hide) {
            errorContainer.hide();
        }
        if (typeof errorSelector !== 'undefined' && errorSelector.hide) {
            errorSelector.hide();
        }
    }

    function showLoginError(message) {
        if (!message) {
            return;
        }
        if (typeof errorContainer !== 'undefined' && errorContainer.show) {
            errorContainer.show(message);
            return;
        }
        if (typeof errorSelector !== 'undefined' && errorSelector.html) {
            errorSelector.html(message);
            errorSelector.show();
            return;
        }
        alert(message);
    }

    function resolveRefUrl(element) {
        var refUrl = null;
        if (element) {
            refUrl = $(element).closest('form').find("input[name='ref_url']").val();
        }
        if (!refUrl) {
            refUrl = $('input[name=ref_url]').val() || currentCleanPath();
        }
        return normalizeRefUrl(refUrl);
    }

    function startGoogleOAuthRedirect(element, oauthMode) {
        var params = [
            'ref_url=' + encodeURIComponent(resolveRefUrl(element) || '/')
        ];
        if (oauthMode === 'connect') {
            params.push('mode=connect');
        }
        window.location.assign(googleStartPath + '&' + params.join('&'));
    }

    function onLoginSuccess(data) {
        if (typeof registerAuthEvent === 'function') {
            registerAuthEvent('login');
        }
        if (typeof registerAnalyticsEvent === 'function') {
            registerAnalyticsEvent('NTG account', 'login', 'success');
        }
        if (typeof registerMarfeelUserType === 'function') {
            registerMarfeelUserType(data);
        }

        clearGoogleOAuthHash();

        var fallbackUrl = normalizeRefUrl($('input[name=ref_url]').val() || '') || currentCleanPath();
        if (typeof handleAuthLoginRedirect === 'function') {
            handleAuthLoginRedirect(data, fallbackUrl);
            return;
        }
        window.location.replace(fallbackUrl);
    }

    function submitGoogleLoginToken(token) {
        $.ajax({
            type: 'post',
            url: '/',
            data: {
                module: 'users',
                action: 'googleLogin',
                token: token,
                ajax: 1,
                ref_url: normalizeRefUrl($('input[name=ref_url]').val() || '') || currentCleanPath()
            },
            success: function (data) {
                if (data && data.success) {
                    onLoginSuccess(data);
                } else {
                    var message = (data && data.messages && data.messages.error)
                        ? data.messages.error
                        : (data && data.message ? data.message : null);
                    showLoginError(message);
                }
            },
            error: function () {
                showLoginError('Google login failed.');
            }
        });
    }

    function handleCredentialResponse(response) {
        if (!response || !response.credential) {
            return;
        }
        if (mode === 'connect') {
            return;
        }
        submitGoogleLoginToken(response.credential);
    }

    function initOneTap() {
        if (mode !== 'login' || /firefox/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '')) {
            return;
        }
        if (!window.google || !google.accounts || !google.accounts.id) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            google.accounts.id.initialize({
                client_id: clientId,
                callback: handleCredentialResponse,
                auto_select: false,
                cancel_on_tap_outside: true,
                use_fedcm_for_prompt: true,
                context: 'signin'
            });
            google.accounts.id.prompt();
        } catch (e) {
            // One Tap is optional.
        }
    }

    function loadGisForOneTap() {
        if (mode !== 'login' || /firefox/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '')) {
            return;
        }
        if (window.google && google.accounts && google.accounts.id) {
            initOneTap();
            return;
        }
        var existing = document.querySelector('script[data-bn-gsi="1"]');
        if (existing) {
            existing.addEventListener('load', initOneTap);
            return;
        }
        var script = document.createElement('script');
        script.src = 'https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client';
        script.async = true;
        script.defer = true;
        script.setAttribute('data-bn-gsi', '1');
        script.onload = initOneTap;
        document.head.appendChild(script);
    }

    window.loginGoogle = function (element, event) {
        if (event && typeof event.preventDefault === 'function') {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
        }
        if (window.ReactNativeWebView) {
            window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify({ action: 'login', data: { type: 'google' } }));
            return false;
        }

        hideLoginError();
        startGoogleOAuthRedirect(element, 'login');
        return false;
    };

    if (mode === 'connect') {
        $('#disconnect-google').on('click', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!window.confirm(disconnectConfirm)) {
                return;
            }
            $.post('/index.php?module=users&action=linkSocialAccount', {
                account_type: 'google',
                account_action: 'disconnect',
                token: null
            }, function () {
                setGoogleLinkState(false);
            });
        });

        $('#connect-google').on('click', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            startGoogleOAuthRedirect(this, 'connect');
        });
    }

    window.bnGoogleAuth = {
        mode: mode,
        start: startGoogleOAuthRedirect
    };

    clearGoogleOAuthHash();
    loadGisForOneTap();
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
    if (!(window.history && window.history.replaceState)) {
        return;
    }

    var path = window.location.pathname || '/';
    var hash = window.location.hash || '';
    var search = window.location.search || '';
    var nextSearch = search;
    var dirty = false;

    if (hash.indexOf('id_token=') !== -1 || hash.indexOf('iss=') !== -1) {
        dirty = true;
        hash = '';
    }

    if (search) {
        try {
            var params = new URLSearchParams(search);
            var ref = params.get('ref_url') || '';
            var decodedRef = '';
            try {
                decodedRef = decodeURIComponent(ref);
            } catch (e) {
                decodedRef = ref;
            }
            // Only strip clearly nested oauth leftovers, e.g. ref_url=/?ref_url=%2F
            if (ref.indexOf('ref_url=') !== -1 || decodedRef.indexOf('ref_url=') !== -1) {
                params.delete('ref_url');
                dirty = true;
                var cleaned = params.toString();
                nextSearch = cleaned ? ('?' + cleaned) : '';
            }
        } catch (e) {
            // ignore
        }
    }

    if (!dirty) {
        return;
    }

    window.history.replaceState(null, '', path + nextSearch);
})();
</script>

<style>
    .navbar-mobile-alt-btn.active .navbar-mobile-alt-svg {
        --Style: url(#bn-nav-icon-active);
    }

    .navbar-mobile-alt-btn.active .navbar-mobile-alt-svg path {
        fill: var(--Style, linear-gradient(270deg, #DB6600 0%, #F78222 100%));
    }
</style>
<script>
window.BN_DATE_LOCALE = window.BN_DATE_LOCALE || {"months":[null,"January","February","March","April","May","June","July","August","September","October","November","December"],"days":["Sunday","Monday","Tuesday","Wednesday","Thursday","Friday","Saturday"],"days_short":["Sun","Mon","Tue","Wed","Thu","Fri","Sat"],"today":"Today","tomorrow":"Tomorrow","day_after_tomorrow":"Day after tomorrow"};
</script>

<script>
    $('.navbar-mobile').detach().insertAfter('#header');
    $('.navbar-mobile-alt').detach().appendTo('body');

    var $altNav = $('.navbar-mobile-alt');
    var $altBtns = $altNav.find('.navbar-mobile-alt-btn');
    var $altNavOffcanvasAlt = $('#offcanvasNavbarMobileAlt');
    var $prevActiveBtn = null;

    function setAltNavActive(btn) {
        $altBtns.removeClass('active');
        $(btn).addClass('active');
        $altNav[0].style.setProperty('--tab-active', $altBtns.index(btn));
        $altNav.find('.navbar-mobile-alt-slide').css('visibility', 'visible');
    }

    $altBtns.on('click', function() {
        var offcanvasEl = $altNavOffcanvasAlt[0];
        if (offcanvasEl && offcanvasEl.classList.contains('show')) return;
        var triggersOffcanvas = this.dataset.bsToggle === 'offcanvas';
        if (triggersOffcanvas) {
            var currentActive = $altBtns.filter('.active')[0] || null;
            if (currentActive && currentActive !== this) $prevActiveBtn = currentActive;
        }
        setAltNavActive(this);
    });

    var xpShopLoaded = false;
    var xpShopLoading = false;
    var xpShopHashSyncing = false;

    function isShopHash() {
        return (window.location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '') === 'shop';
    }

    function setShopHash(enabled) {
        if (xpShopHashSyncing) return;
        if (enabled === isShopHash()) return;
        var base = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
        xpShopHashSyncing = true;
        if (window.history && window.history.replaceState) {
            window.history.replaceState(null, '', enabled ? base + '#shop' : base);
        } else if (enabled) {
            window.location.hash = 'shop';
        } else {
            window.location.hash = '';
        }
        xpShopHashSyncing = false;
    }

    function ensureAltOffcanvasOpen(done, attempt) {
        var el = $altNavOffcanvasAlt[0];
        if (!el) {
            if (done) done();
            return;
        }
        var menuBtn = $altBtns.filter('[data-bs-target="#offcanvasNavbarMobileAlt"]')[0];
        if (menuBtn) setAltNavActive(menuBtn);
        if (el.classList.contains('show')) {
            if (done) done();
            return;
        }
        if (typeof bootstrap === 'undefined' || !bootstrap.Offcanvas) {
            if ((attempt || 0) < 20) {
                window.setTimeout(function() {
                    ensureAltOffcanvasOpen(done, (attempt || 0) + 1);
                }, 50);
                return;
            }
            if (done) done();
            return;
        }
        var inst = bootstrap.Offcanvas.getOrCreateInstance(el);
        if (done) {
            el.addEventListener('shown.bs.offcanvas', function onShown() {
                el.removeEventListener('shown.bs.offcanvas', onShown);
                done();
            });
        }
        inst.show();
    }

    function closeXpTiersPanel() {
        var panel = document.getElementById('xp-tiers-panel');
        if (!panel) return;
        panel.hidden = true;
        panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        $altNavOffcanvasAlt.removeClass('xp-tiers-open');
        if (typeof window.bnXpTiersCloseHowItWorks === 'function') {
            window.bnXpTiersCloseHowItWorks();
        }
    }

    function openXpTiersPanel() {
        closeXpShopPanel();
        var panel = document.getElementById('xp-tiers-panel');
        if (!panel) return;
        panel.hidden = false;
        panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        $altNavOffcanvasAlt.addClass('xp-tiers-open');
        panel.scrollTop = 0;
    }

    function closeXpShopPanel() {
        var panel = document.getElementById('xp-shop-panel');
        if (!panel) return;
        panel.hidden = true;
        panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        $altNavOffcanvasAlt.removeClass('xp-shop-open');
        if (typeof window.bnXpShopCloseHowItWorks === 'function') {
            window.bnXpShopCloseHowItWorks();
        }
        setShopHash(false);
    }

    function showXpShopPanel() {
        var panel = document.getElementById('xp-shop-panel');
        if (!panel) return;
        panel.hidden = false;
        panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
        $altNavOffcanvasAlt.addClass('xp-shop-open');
        panel.scrollTop = 0;
        setShopHash(true);
    }

    function openXpShopPanel() {
        closeXpTiersPanel();
        var panel = document.getElementById('xp-shop-panel');
        if (!panel) return;

        if (xpShopLoaded) {
            showXpShopPanel();
            return;
        }
        if (xpShopLoading) {
            showXpShopPanel();
            return;
        }

        var url = panel.getAttribute('data-xp-shop-partial-url');
        if (!url) return;

        xpShopLoading = true;
        panel.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
        showXpShopPanel();

        $.ajax({
            url: url,
            method: 'GET',
            dataType: 'html',
            headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
        }).done(function(html) {
            $(panel).html(html);
            xpShopLoaded = true;
        }).fail(function() {
            panel.innerHTML = '<p class="xp-shop__empty">Failed to load shop</p>';
        }).always(function() {
            xpShopLoading = false;
            panel.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
        });
    }

    function openXpShopFromHash() {
        if (!document.getElementById('xp-shop-panel')) return;
        ensureAltOffcanvasOpen(function() {
            openXpShopPanel();
        });
    }

    $(document).on('click', '[data-xp-tiers-open]', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        openXpTiersPanel();
    });

    $(document).on('click', '[data-xp-tiers-close]', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        closeXpTiersPanel();
    });

    $(document).on('click', '[data-xp-shop-open]', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        openXpShopPanel();
    });

    $(document).on('click', '[data-xp-shop-close]', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        closeXpShopPanel();
    });

    window.addEventListener('hashchange', function() {
        if (xpShopHashSyncing) return;
        if (isShopHash()) {
            openXpShopFromHash();
        } else {
            closeXpShopPanel();
        }
    });

    if (isShopHash()) {
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', openXpShopFromHash);
        } else {
            openXpShopFromHash();
        }
    }

    if ($altNavOffcanvasAlt.length) {
        $altNavOffcanvasAlt[0].addEventListener('hidden.bs.offcanvas', function() {
            closeXpTiersPanel();
            closeXpShopPanel();
            if ($prevActiveBtn) {
                setAltNavActive($prevActiveBtn);
                $prevActiveBtn = null;
            } else {
                $altBtns.removeClass('active');
                $altNav.find('.navbar-mobile-alt-slide').css('visibility', 'hidden');
            }
        });
    }

    function getAltNavInitialIndex() {
        var cl = document.body.classList;
        if (cl.contains('page-news2-browse')) return 1;
        if (cl.contains('page-schedules-browseMain')) return 2;
        if (cl.contains('page-video5-allPodcasts') || cl.contains('page-video5-browse')) return 3;
        if (cl.contains('page--')) return 0;
        return -1;
    }

    if ($altBtns.length) { var _idx = getAltNavInitialIndex(); if (_idx >= 0) setAltNavActive($altBtns[_idx]); }

    (function initAltNavCompactOnScroll() {
        var COMPACT_SCROLL_Y = 350;
        var EXPAND_SCROLL_UP = 250;
        var altNavEl = $altNav[0];
        if (!altNavEl) return;

        var lastScrollY = getAltNavScrollY();
        var scrollUpAccum = 0;
        var isCompact = false;
        var ticking = false;

        function getAltNavScrollY() {
            return window.pageYOffset
                || document.documentElement.scrollTop
                || document.body.scrollTop
                || 0;
        }

        function setAltNavCompact(nextCompact) {
            if (isCompact === nextCompact) return;
            isCompact = nextCompact;
            altNavEl.classList.toggle('navbar-mobile-alt--compact', isCompact);
        }

        function updateAltNavCompact() {
            var scrollY = getAltNavScrollY();
            var delta = scrollY - lastScrollY;

            if (scrollY <= COMPACT_SCROLL_Y) {
                setAltNavCompact(false);
                scrollUpAccum = 0;
            } else if (delta > 0) {
                scrollUpAccum = 0;
                setAltNavCompact(true);
            } else if (delta < 0) {
                scrollUpAccum += Math.abs(delta);
                if (scrollUpAccum >= EXPAND_SCROLL_UP) {
                    setAltNavCompact(false);
                    scrollUpAccum = 0;
                }
            }

            lastScrollY = scrollY;
            ticking = false;
        }

        function queueAltNavCompactUpdate() {
            if (ticking) return;
            ticking = true;
            window.requestAnimationFrame(updateAltNavCompact);
        }

        window.addEventListener('scroll', queueAltNavCompactUpdate, { passive: true });
        document.addEventListener('scroll', queueAltNavCompactUpdate, { passive: true });
        window.addEventListener('resize', queueAltNavCompactUpdate, { passive: true });
        queueAltNavCompactUpdate();
    })();

    $(document).on('submit', '#offcanvas-alt-search-form', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var query = $('#offcanvas-alt-search-input').val().trim();
        if (!query) return;
        // var offcanvasInstance = bootstrap.Offcanvas.getInstance(document.getElementById('offcanvasNavbarMobileAlt'));
        // if (offcanvasInstance) offcanvasInstance.hide();
        if (typeof bnRunGoogleCseSearch === 'function') {
            bnRunGoogleCseSearch(query);
            return;
        }
        $('#search-modal').modal();
        setTimeout(function() {
            $('#gsc-i-id1').val(query).focus();
            $('.gsc-search-button').click();
        }, 100);
    });

    if (navigator && navigator.userAgent && navigator.userAgent.includes('_app')) {
        $('.mobile-link').removeClass('d-none');
        $('.js-app-only-nav').removeClass('d-none');
        $('body').addClass('app-webview');
        $('.navbar-mobile a[target="_blank"]').removeAttr('target');
    }

    $(document).ready(function() {
        if (typeof generateListForMenuSchedule !== 'undefined') {
            const list = generateListForMenuSchedule('#schedulesListMobileNav .schedule-table-row', '<div class="schedules-list-wrapper schedules-list-wrapper-mobile-menu">');
            $('#schedulesListMobileNav').html(list);
        }
    });

    $('body').on('click', '.mobile-link', () => {
        if (navigator.userAgent.includes('ios_app')) {
            if (window.webkit && window.webkit.messageHandlers && window.webkit.messageHandlers.iosApp) {
                window.webkit.messageHandlers.iosApp.postMessage("openSettings");
            }
        }
        if (navigator.userAgent.includes('android_app')) {
            if (typeof androidApp !== 'undefined' && androidApp && androidApp.openSettings) {
                androidApp.openSettings();
            }
        }
        if (window.ReactNativeWebView) {
            window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify({ action: 'navigateToSettingsScreen' }));
            return;
        }
    });

    // $('body').on('click', '.nav-link-mobile-search', () => {
    //     var $mobileSearchInput = $('.mobile-search .form-control:visible');
    //     if ($mobileSearchInput.length) {
    //         setTimeout(() => {
    //             $mobileSearchInput.focus();
    //         }, 100);
    //     }
    // });

    function applyOffcanvasWebviewNestedScrollingWorkaround(el, extraBodyClass) {
        if (!el) return;
        var documentScrollTop = 0,
            extraScroll = 800;

        el.addEventListener('show.bs.offcanvas', function () {
            $('body').addClass('mobile-nav-on');
            if (extraBodyClass) $('body').addClass(extraBodyClass);
        });
        el.addEventListener('shown.bs.offcanvas', function () {
            if ($('body').hasClass('app-webview')) {
                documentScrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
                if (documentScrollTop < extraScroll) {
                    window.scrollTo({
                        top: documentScrollTop + extraScroll,
                        behavior: 'instant',
                    });
                }
            }
        });
        el.addEventListener('hide.bs.offcanvas', function () {
            $('body').removeClass('mobile-nav-on');
            if (extraBodyClass) $('body').removeClass(extraBodyClass);

            if ($('body').hasClass('app-webview')) {
                if ($(window).scrollTop() !== documentScrollTop) {
                    window.scrollTo({
                        top: documentScrollTop,
                        behavior: 'instant',
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    }

    applyOffcanvasWebviewNestedScrollingWorkaround(document.getElementById('offcanvasNavbarMobile'), 'mobile-nav-start-on');
    applyOffcanvasWebviewNestedScrollingWorkaround(document.getElementById('offcanvasNavbarMobileAlt'));
</script>

<script>
    let modalRecaptchaId;
    let recaptchaModalLoading = false;

    function getVisibleModalRecaptchaEl() {
        return document.querySelector('#mainSiteModal.is-visible #modal_recaptcha');
    }

    function ensureModalRecaptcha() {
        if (modalRecaptchaId !== undefined || recaptchaModalLoading) {
            return;
        }

        const el = getVisibleModalRecaptchaEl();
        if (!el) {
            return;
        }

        recaptchaModalLoading = true;

        window.onloadCallbackModal = function () {
            recaptchaModalLoading = false;
            const target = getVisibleModalRecaptchaEl();
            if (!target || modalRecaptchaId !== undefined) {
                return;
            }

            target.innerHTML = '';
            modalRecaptchaId = grecaptcha.render(target, {
                sitekey: '6Le9wE0bAAAAAGKW305kKEgnk-zh1g3cADSOf02W',
                isolated: true,
            });
        };

        const script = document.createElement('script');
        script.src = 'https://www.google.com/recaptcha/api.js?onload=onloadCallbackModal&render=explicit';
        script.async = true;
        script.defer = true;
        document.head.appendChild(script);
    }

    $(document).ready(function() {
        const registerHeader = $('.auth__header--register');
        const loginHeader = $('.auth__header--login');
        const registerForm = $('.auth__form--register');
        const loginForm = $('.auth__form--login');

        loginHeader.on('click', '.btn', function() {
            showRegisterForm();
        });

        registerHeader.on('click', '.btn', function() {
            showLoginForm();
        });

        function showRegisterForm() {
            registerHeader.show();
            registerForm.show();
            registerHeader.addClass('active');
            loginHeader.hide();
            loginForm.hide();
            loginHeader.removeClass('active');
            ensureModalRecaptcha();
        }

        function showLoginForm() {
            loginHeader.show();
            loginForm.show();
            loginHeader.addClass('active');
            registerHeader.hide();
            registerForm.hide();
            registerHeader.removeClass('active');
        }

        
        registerForm.on('submit', '#register-form-modal', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const formObj = $(this);
            const url = "/index.php";
            const submitButton = formObj.find('button[type="submit"]');

            submitButton.prop('disabled', true);
            formObj.addClass('loading');

            $('#register-username-modal, #register-pw-modal, #modal_recaptcha')
            .removeClass('invalid-form-field')
            .next('.invalid-form-field-message').remove();

            $('#register-pw-modal')
            .removeClass('invalid-form-field').parent()
            .next('.invalid-form-field-message').remove();

            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: url,
                data: formObj.serialize(),
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data) {
                    if (data) {
                        if (data.redirect) {
                            registerAuthEvent('registration');
                            window.location.href = data.redirect;
                        }

                        if (data.messages?.error) {
                            for (const key in data.messages.error) {
                                let errorMessage = data.messages.error[key];

                                
                                    switch (key) {
                                        case 'invalid-email':
                                        case 'invalid-email-exists':
                                            $('#register-username-modal').addClass('invalid-form-field');
                                            $('#register-username-modal').after(`<div class="invalid-form-field-message">${errorMessage}</div>`);
                                            break;
                                        case 'invalid-password':
                                            $('#register-pw-modal').addClass('invalid-form-field');
                                            $('#register-pw-modal').parent().after(`<div class="invalid-form-field-message">${errorMessage}</div>`);
                                            break;
                                        case 'invalid-captcha':
                                            $('#modal_recaptcha').addClass('invalid-form-field');
                                            $('#modal_recaptcha').after(`<div class="invalid-form-field-message">${errorMessage}</div>`);
                                            break;
                                        default:
                                            break;
                                    }
                                
                            }

                            if (modalRecaptchaId !== undefined) {
                                grecaptcha.reset(modalRecaptchaId);
                            }
                        }

                        submitButton.prop('disabled', false);
                        formObj.removeClass('loading');
                    }
                },
            });
        });
    });

    $(document).on('click', '.mainSiteModalButton[data-content-holder="#login_form"]', function () {
        setTimeout(function () {
            if ($('#mainSiteModal .auth__form--register').is(':visible')) {
                ensureModalRecaptcha();
            }
        }, 250);
    });

    $(document).on('click', '#mainSiteModal .auth__header--login .btn', function () {
        setTimeout(ensureModalRecaptcha, 0);
    });

    $('.auth-modal .show-password').click(function() {
        const passwordInput = $(this).siblings('input[type="password"], input[type="text"]');
        const eyeIcon = $(this).find('img');
        const isVisible = passwordInput.attr('type') === 'text';

        passwordInput.attr('type', isVisible ? 'password' : 'text');
        eyeIcon.attr({
            src: isVisible ?
                '/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/icons/eye-gray.svg' :
                '/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/icons/eye-off-gray.svg',
            alt: isVisible
            ? "Show password" 
            : "Hide password",
            title: isVisible
            ? "Show password" 
            : "Hide password"
        });
    });
</script>
                            </div>
                                            </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

<style>
    .navbar-nav-main .nav-item-has-dropdown {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 1060;
    }
    @media (min-width: 992px) {
        .navbar-nav-main .nav-item-has-dropdown:hover > .dropdown-menu {
            display: block;
        }

        .user-menu-dropdown > .dropdown-menu {
            --bs-dropdown-spacer: 0;
        }

        .user-menu-dropdown:hover > .dropdown-menu,
        .user-menu-dropdown:focus-within > .dropdown-menu {
            display: block;
        }

        .user-menu-dropdown:hover .profile-img-wrapper,
        .user-menu-dropdown:focus-within .profile-img-wrapper {
            border-color: var(--bs-primary);
        }
    }
</style>
<script>
    $(document).ready(function () {
        function closeExpandableMenu() {
            if ($(".expandable_menu_button:hover, .expandable-menu-block:hover").length === 0) {
                $('.expandable_menu_button').removeClass('active');
                $('.expandable-menu-block').addClass('hidden');
            }
        }

        var body = $('body'),
            menuTimeout;

        body.on('mouseout', '.expandable_menu_button, .expandable-menu-block', function (e) {
            clearTimeout(menuTimeout);
            setTimeout(closeExpandableMenu, 200)
        });

        body.on('mouseover', '.expandable_menu_button', function (e) {
            if ($(this).hasClass('nav-item-has-dropdown') || $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').length) {
                return;
            }
            var menuTimeoutMiliseconds = $(".expandable-menu-block:visible").length > 0 ? 50 : 500;

            menuTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
                $('.expandable_menu_button').not('.nav-item-has-dropdown').removeClass('active');
                $('.expandable-menu-block').addClass('hidden');

                var $dropdownButton = $(e.currentTarget);
                $dropdownButton.addClass('active');

                if ($dropdownButton.hasClass('news_list_button')) {
                    $('.expandable-menu-news').removeClass('hidden');
                }
                // Disabled: schedules_list_button / results_list_button / bn_plus_menu_button
                // are not currently in the desktop site menu (display_desktop=0).
                // } else if ($dropdownButton.hasClass('schedules_list_button')) {
                //     $('.expandable-menu-schedules').removeClass('hidden');
                //     $('.results-list-wrapper').css('display', 'none');
                //     $('.schedules-list-wrapper').css('display', 'block');
                //     $('.results_link').addClass('hidden');
                //     $('.schedule_link').removeClass('hidden');
                //     $('.tv-list-wrapper').removeClass('hidden');
                // } else if ($dropdownButton.hasClass('results_list_button')) {
                //     $('.expandable-menu-schedules').removeClass('hidden');
                //     $('.schedules-list-wrapper').css('display', 'none');
                //     $('.results-list-wrapper').css('display', 'block');
                //     $('.results_link').removeClass('hidden');
                //     $('.schedule_link').addClass('hidden');
                //     $('.tv-list-wrapper').addClass('hidden');
                // } else if ($dropdownButton.hasClass('bn_plus_menu_button')) {
                //     $('.expandable-menu-bn-plus').removeClass('hidden');
                // }
            }, menuTimeoutMiliseconds);
        });

        var $navDropdownItems = $('.navbar-nav-main .nav-item-has-dropdown');
        $navDropdownItems.on('mouseenter', function () {
            var toggle = this.querySelector('[data-bs-toggle="dropdown"]');
            if (toggle && typeof bootstrap !== 'undefined' && bootstrap.Dropdown) {
                bootstrap.Dropdown.getOrCreateInstance(toggle).show();
            } else {
                $(this).find('> .dropdown-menu').addClass('show');
                $(toggle).addClass('show').attr('aria-expanded', 'true');
            }
        });
        $navDropdownItems.on('mouseleave', function () {
            var toggle = this.querySelector('[data-bs-toggle="dropdown"]');
            if (toggle && typeof bootstrap !== 'undefined' && bootstrap.Dropdown) {
                var dropdown = bootstrap.Dropdown.getInstance(toggle);
                if (dropdown) {
                    dropdown.hide();
                }
            } else {
                $(this).find('> .dropdown-menu').removeClass('show');
                $(toggle).removeClass('show').attr('aria-expanded', 'false');
            }
        });

        var logoMenuOpenTimer, logoMenuCloseTimer;

        body.on('mouseenter', '#header .logo-dropdown', function () {
            clearTimeout(logoMenuCloseTimer);
            var dropdown = $(this);
            logoMenuOpenTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                dropdown.addClass('open');
            }, 500);
        });

        body.on('mouseleave', '#header .logo-dropdown', function () {
            clearTimeout(logoMenuOpenTimer);
            var dropdown = $(this);
            logoMenuCloseTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                dropdown.removeClass('open');
            }, 200);
        });

        body.on('click', '#other_menu', function () {
            clearTimeout(menuTimeout);

            if ($(this).hasClass('active')) {
                $('#other_menu').removeClass('active');
                $('#second_menu').css('visibility', 'hidden');
                $('.hamburger').removeClass('open');
            } else {
                $('#other_menu').addClass('active');
                $('#second_menu').css('visibility', 'visible');
                $('.hamburger').addClass('open');
            }
        });

        function showSearchBackdrop() {
            $('#navbar-search-container').removeClass("d-none");
            // Copy google search value to our search box
            if ($('#gsc-i-id1').val()) {
                $('#navbar-search-container-input').val($('#gsc-i-id1').val());
            }
            $('#navbar-search-container-input').focus();
            $('.menu-backdrop').removeClass('d-none');
        }

        function hideSearchBackdrop() {
            $('#navbar-search-container').addClass("d-none");
            $('.menu-backdrop').addClass('d-none');
        }

        body.on('click', '#search-modal-button', function () {
            if ($('.menu-backdrop').hasClass("d-none")) {
                showSearchBackdrop();
            } else {
                hideSearchBackdrop();
            }
        });

        body.on('click', '#navbar-search-container .close-icon, .menu-backdrop', function () {
            hideSearchBackdrop();
        });

        body.on('submit', '#navbar-search-container form', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            hideSearchBackdrop();
            var query = $('#navbar-search-container-input').val();
            if (typeof bnRunGoogleCseSearch === 'function') {
                bnRunGoogleCseSearch(query);
                return;
            }
            $('#search-modal').modal();
            setTimeout(() => {
                $('#gsc-i-id1').val(query).focus();
                $('.gsc-search-button').click();
            }, 100);
        });

        // Disabled with schedules mega-menu (schedules_list_button / results_list_button not in desktop menu).
        // if (typeof generateListForMenuSchedule !== 'undefined') {
        //     const list = generateListForMenuSchedule('#schedulesListDesktopNav .schedule-table-row', '<div class="schedule-list-desktop-wrapper">');
        //     $('#schedulesListDesktopNav').html(list);
        //     const tvList = generateListForMenuSchedule('#schedulesTvListDesktopNav .schedule-table-row', '<div class="schedule-list-desktop-wrapper">');
        //     $('#schedulesTvListDesktopNav').html(tvList);
        // }
    });
</script>

        <div class="high-content-wrapper flex-fill overflow-hidden">
                                                    
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="/includes/swiper/swiper-bundle.min.css">
    <div class="games_schedules_results_block with_announcement">
                <div class="prevPage"><span></span></div>
        <div class="frame swiper games-schedules-swiper" id="cyclepages" data-start-cycling-at="0">
            <ul class="clearfix scroll_games_list swiper-wrapper">
                            </ul>
        </div>
        
        <script type="text/javascript">
            (function () {
                window.bnGamesSchedulesCarousel = {
                    getStartAt: function (frame) {
                        var base = parseInt(frame.getAttribute('data-start-cycling-at'), 10) || 0;
                        var value = window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--bs-breakpoint-sm');
                        var isSmUp = window.matchMedia('(min-width: ' + value + ')').matches;
                        return base + (isSmUp ? 0 : 3);
                    },
                    getSlideOffset: function (frame, slideIndex) {
                        var wrapper = frame.querySelector('.swiper-wrapper');
                        if (!wrapper) {
                            return 0;
                        }

                        var slides = wrapper.children;
                        slideIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(slideIndex, slides.length - 1));
                        return slides[slideIndex] ? slides[slideIndex].offsetLeft : 0;
                    },
                    applyPosition: function (frame, swiper, slideIndex, speed) {
                        var offset = this.getSlideOffset(frame, slideIndex);
                        var wrapper = frame.querySelector('.swiper-wrapper');

                        if (swiper) {
                            swiper.setTransition(speed || 0);
                            swiper.setTranslate(-offset);
                            swiper.updateActiveIndex(slideIndex);
                            swiper.updateSlidesClasses();
                            if (speed) {
                                swiper.setTransition(300);
                            }
                            return;
                        }

                        if (wrapper) {
                            wrapper.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + (-offset) + 'px, 0, 0)';
                        }
                    },
                    init: function () {
                        var frame = document.getElementById('cyclepages');
                        if (!frame) {
                            return;
                        }
                        this.applyPosition(frame, null, this.getStartAt(frame), 0);
                    }
                };

                window.bnGamesSchedulesCarousel.init();
            }());
        </script>
        
        <div class="nextPage"><span></span></div>
        <div class="me-2 h-100 d-flex">
            <a href="/games.html" class="btn btn-outline-gray-light rounded-3 text-uppercase ff-alt fw-medium ls-nsm fs-xs lh-xs h-100 d-flex flex-column align-items-center justify-content-center gap-1 px-3 games-shortcut-btn">
            <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M17 12h-5v5h5v-5zM16 1v2H8V1H6v2H5c-1.11 0-1.99.9-1.99 2L3 19c0 1.1.89 2 2 2h14c1.1 0 2-.9 2-2V5c0-1.1-.9-2-2-2h-1V1h-2zm3 18H5V8h14v11z"/></svg>
            <span>Schedule</span>
        </a>

    <script src="/includes/swiper/swiper-bundle.min.js"></script>
    
    <script type="text/javascript">
        (function () {
            var carousel = window.bnGamesSchedulesCarousel;

            function positionCarousel(frame, swiper, speed) {
                if (!carousel) {
                    return;
                }
                carousel.applyPosition(frame, swiper, carousel.getStartAt(frame), speed || 0);
            }

            function initGamesSchedulesSwiper() {
                if (typeof Swiper === 'undefined') {
                    return setTimeout(initGamesSchedulesSwiper, 50);
                }

                var frame = document.getElementById('cyclepages');
                if (!frame || frame.dataset.bnSwiperInited === '1') {
                    return;
                }

                var wrap = frame.parentElement;
                var startAt = carousel ? carousel.getStartAt(frame) : 0;

                positionCarousel(frame, null, 0);

                var swiper = new Swiper(frame, {
                    slidesPerView: 'auto',
                    spaceBetween: 0,
                    speed: 300,
                    grabCursor: true,
                    resistanceRatio: 0.85,
                    observer: true,
                    observeParents: true,
                    autoplay: {
                        delay: 1000,
                        pauseOnMouseEnter: true,
                        disableOnInteraction: false
                    },
                    on: {
                        init: function (instance) {
                            positionCarousel(frame, instance, 0);
                            if (instance.autoplay) {
                                instance.autoplay.stop();
                            }
                        },
                        resize: function (instance) {
                            positionCarousel(frame, instance, 0);
                        }
                    }
                });

                var prevBtn = wrap.querySelector('.prevPage');
                if (prevBtn) {
                    prevBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        carousel.applyPosition(frame, swiper, Math.max(0, swiper.activeIndex - 2), 300);
                    });
                }

                var nextBtn = wrap.querySelector('.nextPage');
                if (nextBtn) {
                    nextBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        carousel.applyPosition(
                            frame,
                            swiper,
                            Math.min(swiper.slides.length - 1, swiper.activeIndex + 2),
                            300
                        );
                    });
                }

                wrap.querySelectorAll('.pause').forEach(function (btn) {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        if (swiper.autoplay) {
                            swiper.autoplay.stop();
                        }
                    });
                });

                wrap.querySelectorAll('.resume').forEach(function (btn) {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        if (swiper.autoplay) {
                            swiper.autoplay.start();
                        }
                    });
                });

                wrap.querySelectorAll('.toggle').forEach(function (btn) {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                        if (!swiper.autoplay) {
                            return;
                        }
                        if (swiper.autoplay.running) {
                            swiper.autoplay.stop();
                        } else {
                            swiper.autoplay.start();
                        }
                    });
                });

                frame.dataset.bnSwiperInited = '1';
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initGamesSchedulesSwiper);
            } else {
                initGamesSchedulesSwiper();
            }
        }());
    </script>
