<script>
(function () {
    var podcastAjaxUrl = '/index.php?module=video5&action=podcastEpisodesAjax';
    var podcastAllUrlTpl = '/video/all/__PODCAST__.html';
    var labels = {
        labelNew: 'New',
        labelPopular: 'Popular'
    };

    
    function formatDuration(seconds) {
        if (!seconds) return '';
        var h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
        var m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
        var s = seconds % 60;
        var mm = m < 10 ? '0' + m : m;
        var ss = s < 10 ? '0' + s : s;
        return h ? h + ':' + mm + ':' + ss : m + ':' + ss;
    }

    function escHtml(s) {
        return String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
    }

    function updateNavButtons(targetId) {
        var track = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (!track) return;
        var atStart = track.scrollLeft <= 0;
        var atEnd = track.scrollLeft >= track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth - 1;
        document.querySelectorAll('.stream-podcasts-section__nav-btn[data-target="' + targetId + '"]').forEach(function (btn) {
            var dir = btn.getAttribute('data-dir');
            btn.classList.toggle('is-disabled', (dir === 'prev' && atStart) || (dir === 'next' && atEnd));
        });
        var outer = track.parentElement;
        if (outer) {
            outer.classList.toggle('is-at-start', atStart);
            outer.classList.toggle('is-at-end', atEnd);
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var btn = e.target.closest('.stream-podcasts-section__nav-btn');
        if (!btn) return;
        var targetId = btn.getAttribute('data-target');
        var dir = btn.getAttribute('data-dir');
        var track = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (!track) return;
        var sample = track.querySelector('.stream-podcasts-section__show, .stream-podcasts-section__episode');
        var step = sample ? sample.offsetWidth + 16 : 200;
        track.scrollBy({ left: (dir === 'next' ? 1 : -1) * step, behavior: 'smooth' });
        setTimeout(function () { updateNavButtons(targetId); }, 350);
    });

    ['stream-shows', 'stream-episodes'].forEach(function (id) {
        var track = document.getElementById(id);
        if (track) track.addEventListener('scroll', function () { updateNavButtons(id); });
        if (track) setTimeout(function () { updateNavButtons(id); }, 50);
    });

    function renderEpisodes(episodes, showName, podcastUrl) {
        var track = document.getElementById('stream-episodes');
        if (!track) return;
        track.scrollLeft = 0;
        track.innerHTML = '';
        var nameLink = document.getElementById('stream-podcasts-section-active-name-link');
        if (nameLink) {
            nameLink.textContent = showName || '';
            if (podcastUrl) nameLink.setAttribute('href', podcastAllUrlTpl.replace('__PODCAST__', encodeURIComponent(podcastUrl)));
        }
        (episodes || []).forEach(function (ep) {
            var href = '/index.php?module=video5&action=view&id=' + ep.id + '&rec_url_name=' + encodeURIComponent(ep.rec_name || '');
            var img = ep.img_src || '';
            var labelsHtml = '';
            if (ep.is_newest) labelsHtml += '<span class="stream-podcasts-section__badge stream-podcasts-section__badge--new">' + escHtml(labels.labelNew) + '</span>';
            if (ep.is_popular) labelsHtml += '<span class="stream-podcasts-section__badge stream-podcasts-section__badge--popular">' + escHtml(labels.labelPopular) + '</span>';
            if (ep.duration_seconds) labelsHtml += '<span class="stream-podcasts-section__badge stream-podcasts-section__badge--duration">' + formatDuration(ep.duration_seconds) + '</span>';
            track.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
                '<article class="stream-podcasts-section__episode">' +
                    '<a class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-link" href="' + href + '">' +
                        '<div class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-media">' +
                            (img ? '<img class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-img" src="' + img + '" alt="' + escHtml(ep.rec_name || '') + '" loading="lazy" decoding="async"/>' : '') +
                            '<div class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-labels">' + labelsHtml + '</div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-body">' +
                            '<h4 class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-title">' + escHtml(ep.rec_name || '') + '</h4>' +
                            (ep.rec_time && (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - ep.rec_time) < 43200 ? '<time class="stream-podcasts-section__episode-date">' + (ep.ago || '') + '</time>' : '') +
                        '</div>' +
                    '</a>' +
                '</article>'
            );
        });
        updateNavButtons('stream-episodes');
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var btn = e.target.closest('.stream-podcasts-section__show');
        if (!btn) return;
        var url = btn.getAttribute('data-podcast-url');
        var name = btn.getAttribute('data-podcast-name');
        if (!url) return;
        document.querySelectorAll('.stream-podcasts-section__show').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.remove('is-active'); });
        btn.classList.add('is-active');
        var track = document.getElementById('stream-episodes');
        if (track) {
            track.style.opacity = '0.4';
            track.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        }
        fetch(podcastAjaxUrl + '&podcast_url=' + encodeURIComponent(url))
            .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
            .then(function (data) {
                renderEpisodes(data.episodes, data.show ? data.show.name : name, url);
                if (track) {
                    track.style.opacity = '';
                    track.style.pointerEvents = '';
                }
            })
            .catch(function () {
                if (track) {
                    track.style.opacity = '';
                    track.style.pointerEvents = '';
                }
            });
    });
    
})();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    (function () {
        var gameId = 125699;
        try {
            var match = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|;\s*)prediction_home_game_hide=([^;]*)/);
            if (!match) {
                return;
            }
            var hidden = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(match[1]));
            if (!Array.isArray(hidden)) {
                return;
            }
            var isHidden = hidden.some(function (id) {
                return parseInt(id, 10) === gameId;
            });
            if (!isHidden) {
                return;
            }
            var block = document.querySelector('.fantasy-prediction-widget[data-game-id="' + gameId + '"]');
            if (block) {
                block = block.closest('.main_infoblock_1') || block;
                block.parentNode.removeChild(block);
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    })();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        const $widget = $('.fantasy-prediction-widget').first();
        if (!$widget.length) {
            return;
        }

        const gameId = $widget.data('game-id');
        const leagueId = $widget.data('league-id');
        const predictionCookieSuffix = '; domain=.basketnews.com; path=/; SameSite=None; Secure';

        function getHiddenGameIds() {
            let hidden = [];
            try {
                const match = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|;\s*)prediction_home_game_hide=([^;]*)/);
                if (match) {
                    hidden = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(match[1]));
                }
            } catch (e) {}
            if (!Array.isArray(hidden)) {
                return [];
            }

            return hidden
                .map(function (id) { return parseInt(id, 10); })
                .filter(function (id) { return !isNaN(id); });
        }

        function removeWidgetsForGame(targetGameId) {
            $('.fantasy-prediction-widget[data-game-id="' + targetGameId + '"]').each(function () {
                const $block = $(this).closest('.main_infoblock_1');
                ($block.length ? $block : $(this)).remove();
            });
        }

        function savePredictionCookie(data) {
            document.cookie = 'prediction_home_game=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data)) + '; max-age=10800' + predictionCookieSuffix;
        }

        function allWidgets() {
            return $('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
        }

        const PREDICTION_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
        const isResult = $widget.data('is-result') === 1;
        const isNotStarted = $widget.data('not-started') === 1;
        const localTeam1Id = String($widget.data('team1-id'));
        const localTeam2Id = String($widget.data('team2-id'));
        const localScore1 = parseInt($widget.attr('data-points-1'), 10);
        const localScore2 = parseInt($widget.attr('data-points-2'), 10);

        function getPredictionCacheKey(userId, targetGameId) {
            return 'bn_prediction_game_' + userId + '_' + targetGameId;
        }

        function getCachedPredictionResponse(userId, targetGameId) {
            try {
                const raw = localStorage.getItem(getPredictionCacheKey(userId, targetGameId));
                if (!raw) {
                    return null;
                }

                const cached = JSON.parse(raw);
                if (!cached || !cached.ts || !cached.response) {
                    return null;
                }
                if (Date.now() - cached.ts > PREDICTION_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
                    return null;
                }

                // Only reuse long-lived cache once BN already knows the game is final
                if (!isResult) {
                    return null;
                }

                return cached.response;
            } catch (e) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        function savePredictionResponseCache(userId, targetGameId, response) {
            try {
                if (!isResult) {
                    return;
                }

                localStorage.setItem(getPredictionCacheKey(userId, targetGameId), JSON.stringify({
                    ts: Date.now(),
                    response: response,
                }));
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        function loadPrediction() {
            let hasPrediction = false;
            let isFinalScore = isResult;
            let isPredictable = isNotStarted;
            let predictionOutcome = null;
            let winnerTeamId = null;
            let pointsDifference = null;
            let score1 = isResult && !isNaN(localScore1) ? localScore1 : null;
            let score2 = isResult && !isNaN(localScore2) ? localScore2 : null;
            let scoreOffset = null;
            let pickRate1 = null;
            let pickRate2 = null;

            function applyPredictionResponse(response) {
                if (!response.success || !response.data) {
                    return false;
                }

                hasPrediction = false;
                isFinalScore = isResult;
                isPredictable = isNotStarted;
                predictionOutcome = null;
                winnerTeamId = null;
                pointsDifference = null;
                score1 = isResult && !isNaN(localScore1) ? localScore1 : null;
                score2 = isResult && !isNaN(localScore2) ? localScore2 : null;
                scoreOffset = null;
                pickRate1 = null;
                pickRate2 = null;

                const prediction = response.data.prediction;
                const gameResult = response.data.gameResult;

                if (prediction && prediction.winnerTeamId) {
                    hasPrediction = true;
                    winnerTeamId = prediction.winnerTeamId;
                    allWidgets().find('[name="prediction_winner"][value="' + winnerTeamId + '"]').prop('checked', true);
                    allWidgets().each(function () { markPredictedTeam($(this), winnerTeamId); });
                }
                if (prediction && prediction.pointsDifference != null) {
                    pointsDifference = parseInt(prediction.pointsDifference, 10) || 0;
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-score-input').val(pointsDifference);
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status__score').text(pointsDifference);
                    allWidgets().each(function () { updateScoreButtons($(this), pointsDifference); });
                }

                if (isFinalScore) {
                    allWidgets().each(function () {
                        renderFinalScores($(this), { team1Score: score1, team2Score: score2 });
                    });
                    if (!hasPrediction) {
                        predictionOutcome = 'not-predicted';
                    } else {
                        const actualWinner = score1 > score2
                            ? localTeam1Id
                            : score2 > score1
                                ? localTeam2Id
                                : null;
                        const winnerPredicted = actualWinner != null
                            && String(winnerTeamId) === String(actualWinner);
                        if (!winnerPredicted) {
                            predictionOutcome = 'wrong';
                        } else {
                            scoreOffset = Math.abs(Math.abs(score1 - score2) - (pointsDifference || 0));
                            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status__error').text(scoreOffset);
                            if (scoreOffset === 0) predictionOutcome = 'exact';
                            else if (scoreOffset <= 6) predictionOutcome = 'close';
                            else predictionOutcome = 'correct';
                        }
                    }
                } else if (!isPredictable && !hasPrediction) {
                    predictionOutcome = 'not-predicted';
                } else if (gameResult && gameResult.team1PickRate != null) {
                    pickRate1 = Math.round(gameResult.team1PickRate);
                    pickRate2 = Math.round(gameResult.team2PickRate);
                    allWidgets().each(function () { renderPickRates($(this), gameResult); });
                }

                savePredictionCookie({
                    gameId: gameId,
                    hasPrediction: hasPrediction,
                    isFinalScore: isFinalScore,
                    isPredictable: isPredictable,
                    predictionOutcome: predictionOutcome,
                    winnerTeamId: winnerTeamId,
                    pointsDifference: pointsDifference,
                    score1: score1,
                    score2: score2,
                    scoreOffset: scoreOffset,
                    pickRate1: pickRate1,
                    pickRate2: pickRate2,
                });

                return true;
            }

            function finalizePredictionUI() {
                allWidgets().removeClass('fantasy-prediction-widget--bg-loading');
                allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__loader').addClass('d-none');

                if (isFinalScore) {
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__title-wrapper').addClass('d-none');
                } else {
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__title-wrapper').removeClass('d-none');
                }

                if (isFinalScore) {
                    const $status = allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status');
                    $status.removeClass('d-none').addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--final');
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-change').addClass('d-none');
                    if (predictionOutcome) {
                        $status.addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--' + predictionOutcome);
                    }
                } else if ((!isPredictable || isResult) && !hasPrediction) {
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status').removeClass('d-none').addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--not-predicted');
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-change').addClass('d-none');
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form input').prop('disabled', true);
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form button').prop('disabled', true);
                } else if (hasPrediction) {
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form').addClass('d-none');
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status').removeClass('d-none').addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--predicted');
                    if (!isPredictable) {
                        allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-change').addClass('d-none');
                    }
                } else {
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form').removeClass('d-none');
                }
            }

            const userId = parseInt($widget.data('user-id'), 10);
            const cachedResponse = !isNaN(userId) ? getCachedPredictionResponse(userId, gameId) : null;
            if (cachedResponse) {
                applyPredictionResponse(cachedResponse);
                finalizePredictionUI();
                return;
            }

            $.ajax({
                url: $widget.data('get-url'),
                method: 'GET',
                dataType: 'json',
                data: {
                    team1_id: localTeam1Id,
                    team2_id: localTeam2Id,
                },
                success: function (response) {
                    if (applyPredictionResponse(response) && isFinalScore && !isNaN(userId)) {
                        savePredictionResponseCache(userId, gameId, response);
                    }
                },
                complete: finalizePredictionUI
            });
        }

        function markPredictedTeam($w, winnerTeamId) {
            const team1Id = String($w.data('team1-id'));
            const team2Id = String($w.data('team2-id'));
            $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="1"]').toggleClass('team-percent--predicted', String(winnerTeamId) === team1Id);
            $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="2"]').toggleClass('team-percent--predicted', String(winnerTeamId) === team2Id);
        }

        function renderFinalScores($w, gameResult) {
            const score1 = parseInt(gameResult.team1Score, 10);
            const score2 = parseInt(gameResult.team2Score, 10);

            const $team1 = $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="1"]');
            const $team2 = $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="2"]');

            $team1.find('.team-percent__value--percent').addClass('d-none');
            $team2.find('.team-percent__value--percent').addClass('d-none');
            $team1.find('.team-percent__value--score').text(score1).removeClass('d-none');
            $team2.find('.team-percent__value--score').text(score2).removeClass('d-none');

            if (score1 !== score2) {
                $team1.toggleClass('team-percent--winner', score1 > score2);
                $team2.toggleClass('team-percent--winner', score2 > score1);
            }
        }

        function renderPickRates($w, gameResult) {
            const rate1 = Math.round(gameResult.team1PickRate);
            const rate2 = Math.round(gameResult.team2PickRate);

            const $team1 = $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="1"]');
            const $team2 = $w.find('.team-percent[data-team="2"]');

            $team1[0].style.setProperty('--team-percent', rate1 + '%');
            $team1.find('.team-percent__value--score').addClass('d-none');
            $team1.find('.team-percent__value--percent').text(rate1 + '%').removeClass('d-none');

            $team2[0].style.setProperty('--team-percent', rate2 + '%');
            $team2.find('.team-percent__value--score').addClass('d-none');
            $team2.find('.team-percent__value--percent').text(rate2 + '%').removeClass('d-none');
        }

        function updateScoreButtons($w, val) {
            $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-score-minus').prop('disabled', val <= 1);
            $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-score-plus').prop('disabled', val >= 99);
        }

        $(document).on('click', '.fantasy-prediction-widget .fantasy-prediction-score-minus', function () {
            const $w = $(this).closest('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
            const $input = $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-score-input');
            const val = parseInt($input.val(), 10) || 1;
            const newVal = Math.max(1, val - 1);
            $input.val(newVal);
            updateScoreButtons($w, newVal);
        });

        $(document).on('click', '.fantasy-prediction-widget .fantasy-prediction-score-plus', function () {
            const $w = $(this).closest('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
            const $input = $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-score-input');
            const val = parseInt($input.val(), 10) || 0;
            const newVal = Math.min(99, val + 1);
            $input.val(newVal);
            updateScoreButtons($w, newVal);
        });

        $(document).on('click', '.fantasy-prediction-widget .fantasy-prediction-change', function () {
            const $w = $(this).closest('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
            $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-status').addClass('d-none');
            $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-success').addClass('d-none');
            $w.find('.team-percent').removeClass('team-percent--predicted team-percent--winner');
            $w.find('.fantasy-prediction-form').removeClass('d-none');
        });

        $(document).on('submit', '.fantasy-prediction-widget .fantasy-prediction-form', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            const $w = $(this).closest('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
            const gId = $w.data('game-id');
            const lId = $w.data('league-id');
            const winnerTeamId = $w.find('[name="prediction_winner"]:checked').val();
            const pointsDifference = parseInt($w.find('.fantasy-prediction-score-input').val(), 10);

            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-submit').prop('disabled', true);
            allWidgets().addClass('loading');

            $.ajax({
                url: $widget.data('post-url'),
                method: 'POST',
                data: {
                    game_id: gId,
                    league_id: lId,
                    winner_team_id: winnerTeamId,
                    points_difference: pointsDifference,
                },
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function (response) {
                    if (response.success && response.data) {
                        allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form').addClass('d-none');
                        allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status').removeClass('d-none');
                        allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-success').removeClass('d-none');
                        allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status__score').text(pointsDifference);
                        allWidgets().each(function () { markPredictedTeam($(this), winnerTeamId); });
                        const gr = response.data.gameResult;
                        if (gr && gr.team1PickRate != null) allWidgets().each(function () { renderPickRates($(this), gr); });

                        savePredictionCookie({
                            gameId: gameId,
                            hasPrediction: true,
                            isFinalScore: false,
                            isPredictable: true,
                            predictionOutcome: null,
                            winnerTeamId: winnerTeamId,
                            pointsDifference: pointsDifference,
                            score1: null,
                            score2: null,
                            scoreOffset: null,
                            pickRate1: (gr && gr.team1PickRate != null) ? Math.round(gr.team1PickRate) : null,
                            pickRate2: (gr && gr.team2PickRate != null) ? Math.round(gr.team2PickRate) : null,
                        });
                    }
                },
                complete: function () {
                    allWidgets().removeClass('loading');
                    allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-submit').prop('disabled', false);
                }
            });
        });

        $(document).on('click', '.fantasy-prediction-widget .btn-hide-widget', function () {
            const $w = $(this).closest('.fantasy-prediction-widget');
            const gId = parseInt($w.data('game-id'), 10);
            if (isNaN(gId)) {
                return;
            }
            const hidden = getHiddenGameIds();
            if (!hidden.includes(gId)) {
                hidden.push(gId);
                document.cookie = 'prediction_home_game_hide=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(hidden)) + '; max-age=259200' + predictionCookieSuffix;
            }
            removeWidgetsForGame(gId);
        });

        const isGuest = $widget.data('is-guest') === 1;
        if (isGuest && getHiddenGameIds().includes(parseInt(gameId, 10))) {
            removeWidgetsForGame(gameId);
            return;
        }

        if (isGuest && isResult) {
            const score1 = parseInt($widget.attr('data-points-1'), 10);
            const score2 = parseInt($widget.attr('data-points-2'), 10);
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__loader').addClass('d-none');
            allWidgets().each(function () { renderFinalScores($(this), { team1Score: score1, team2Score: score2 }); });
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status').removeClass('d-none')
                .addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--final fantasy-prediction-status--not-predicted');
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-change').addClass('d-none');
        } else if (isGuest && !isNotStarted) {
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__loader').addClass('d-none');
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-status').removeClass('d-none')
                .addClass('fantasy-prediction-status--not-predicted');
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-change').addClass('d-none');
        } else if (isGuest) {
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-widget__loader').addClass('d-none');
            allWidgets().find('.fantasy-prediction-form').removeClass('d-none');
        } else {
            loadPrediction();
        }
    });
</script>

<script>
(function ($) {
    var fantasyStatsWidgetBgUrls = {
        popular: "url('/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/fantasy-bg.png?v2')",
        expensive: "url('/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/fantasy-bg.png?v2')",
        teams: "url('/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/fantasy-bg.png?v2')",
        draft_picks: "url('/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/fantasy-bg.png?v2')"
    };

    function setFantasyStatsView($widget, view) {
        $widget.find('.fantasy-stats-widget__panel').removeClass('is-active');
        $widget.find('.fantasy-stats-widget__panel[data-view="' + view + '"]').addClass('is-active');
        $widget.find('.fantasy-stats-widget__bg')
            .removeClass(function (index, className) {
                return (className.match(/fantasy-stats-widget__bg--\S+/g) || []).join(' ');
            })
            .addClass('fantasy-stats-widget__bg--' + view)
            .css('--fantasy-stats-widget-bg', fantasyStatsWidgetBgUrls[view] || fantasyStatsWidgetBgUrls.popular);
    }

    $('.fantasy-stats-widget').each(function () {
        var initialView = $(this).data('default-view');
        if (initialView) {
            setFantasyStatsView($(this), initialView);
        }
    });

    $(document).on('change', '.fantasy-stats-widget__select', function () {
        setFantasyStatsView($(this).closest('.fantasy-stats-widget'), $(this).val());
    });
})(jQuery);
</script>

    <script>
        (function () {
            function init() {
                if (typeof Swiper === 'undefined') {
                    return setTimeout(init, 50);
                }
                document.querySelectorAll('.news-recommended__swiper').forEach(function (el) {
                    if (el.dataset.bnSwiperInited === '1') return;

                    function buildOrDestroy() {
                        var isDesktop = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 768px)').matches;
                        if (isDesktop) {
                            if (el.swiperInstance) {
                                el.swiperInstance.destroy(true, true);
                                el.swiperInstance = null;
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        if (el.swiperInstance) return;
                        el.swiperInstance = new Swiper(el, {
                            slidesPerView: 'auto',
                            spaceBetween: 16,
                            freeMode: true,
                            watchOverflow: true
                        });
                    }

                    el.dataset.bnSwiperInited = '1';
                    buildOrDestroy();
                    window.addEventListener('resize', buildOrDestroy);
                });
            }
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
            } else {
                init();
            }
        })();
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function initTextContainerScrollDepth(root) {
        var scope = root || document;
        if (!scope.querySelectorAll) {
            return;
        }

        scope.querySelectorAll('.text_container[data-id]').forEach(function (container) {
            if (container.dataset.bnScrollDepthObserved === '1') {
                return;
            }
            container.dataset.bnScrollDepthObserved = '1';

            var name = container.getAttribute('data-name');
            if (!name) {
                return;
            }

            if (window.getComputedStyle(container).position === 'static') {
                container.style.position = 'relative';
            }

            [50, 100].forEach(function (percent) {
                var marker = document.createElement('span');
                marker.className = 'bn-scroll-depth-marker';
                marker.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                marker.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;width:1px;height:1px;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;';
                if (percent === 50) {
                    marker.style.top = '50%';
                } else {
                    marker.style.bottom = '0';
                }
                container.appendChild(marker);

                var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                        if (!entry.isIntersecting) {
                            return;
                        }
                        if (container.offsetHeight < window.innerHeight) {
                            observer.disconnect();
                            return;
                        }
                        registerScrollDepthEvent(percent + '%', name, percent);
                        observer.disconnect();
                    });
                }, { threshold: 0 });

                observer.observe(marker);
            });
        });
    }

    window.BnInitTextContainerScrollDepth = initTextContainerScrollDepth;

    function socialScrollPositionInit() {
        var socialScrollElements = document.querySelectorAll('.social-ul');
        for (var i = 0; i < socialScrollElements.length; i++) {
            socialScrollPosition(socialScrollElements[i]);
        }
    }

    function socialScrollPosition(el) {
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const parent = el.parentNode;
        const parentRect = parent.parentNode.getBoundingClientRect();

        const fixedTop = 173; // Fixed position offset

        // If parent is still in viewport and box should stick
        if (parentRect.top < fixedTop && parentRect.bottom > rect.height + fixedTop) {
            el.style.position = "fixed";
            el.style.top = fixedTop + 'px';
            el.style.bottom = "auto";
        }
        // If box reaches the bottom of the parent, lock it at bottom
        else if (parentRect.bottom <= rect.height + fixedTop) {
            el.style.position = "absolute";
            el.style.top = "auto";
            el.style.bottom = "0";
        }
        // Reset to normal when above sticky zone
        else {
            el.style.position = "absolute";
            el.style.top = "0";
            el.style.bottom = "auto";
        }
    }

    var socialScrollScheduled = false;
    function scheduleSocialScrollPosition() {
        if (socialScrollScheduled) {
            return;
        }
        socialScrollScheduled = true;
        requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            socialScrollScheduled = false;
            socialScrollPositionInit();
        });
    }

    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                initTextContainerScrollDepth();
            });
        } else {
            initTextContainerScrollDepth();
        }
    } else {
        const sentScroll50Events = [];
        const sentScroll100Events = [];
        var legacyScrollDepthTimer = null;

        function trackScrollDepthLegacy() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.text_container[data-id]').forEach(function (el) {
                var id = el.getAttribute('data-id');
                var textHeight = el.offsetHeight;
                var textOffsetToScreenBottom = el.getBoundingClientRect().top - window.innerHeight;
                var textHalf = Math.round(textHeight / 2);
                var name = el.getAttribute('data-name');

                if (textHeight < window.innerHeight || !id || !name) {
                    return;
                }

                if (textOffsetToScreenBottom < 0) {
                    var scrolledPast = Math.abs(textOffsetToScreenBottom);
                    if (textHalf <= scrolledPast && sentScroll50Events.indexOf(id) === -1) {
                        sentScroll50Events.push(id);
                        registerScrollDepthEvent('50%', name, 50);
                    }
                    if (textHeight <= scrolledPast && sentScroll100Events.indexOf(id) === -1) {
                        sentScroll100Events.push(id);
                        registerScrollDepthEvent('100%', name, 100);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        window.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
            clearTimeout(legacyScrollDepthTimer);
            legacyScrollDepthTimer = setTimeout(trackScrollDepthLegacy, 100);
            scheduleSocialScrollPosition();
        }, { passive: true });
    }

    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
        window.addEventListener('scroll', scheduleSocialScrollPosition, { passive: true });
    }

    socialScrollPositionInit();
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
// Init tooltips (after deferred bootstrap.bundle loads)
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    if (typeof bootstrap === 'undefined') {
        return;
    }

    const tooltipTriggerList = document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]');
    [...tooltipTriggerList].map(tooltipTriggerEl => {
        const isDesktop = window.matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches;
        return new bootstrap.Tooltip(tooltipTriggerEl, {
            popperConfig: (defaultBsPopperConfig) => {
                const isFavoritesTooltip = tooltipTriggerEl.getAttribute('data-bs-custom-class') === 'favorites-tooltip-content';
                const isNewsletterTooltip = tooltipTriggerEl.getAttribute('data-bs-custom-class') === 'newsletter-tooltip-content';
                return {
                    ...defaultBsPopperConfig,
                    modifiers: [
                        ...defaultBsPopperConfig.modifiers,
                        {
                            name: 'offset',
                            options: {
                                offset: ({ placement }) => {
                                    if (isFavoritesTooltip && placement === 'top') {
                                        return [-125, 10];
                                    }
                                    return [0, 12];
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        {
                            name: 'flip',
                            options: {
                                fallbackPlacements: isNewsletterTooltip ? ['top'] : ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left']
                            },
                        },
                    ],
                    trigger: isNewsletterTooltip && isDesktop ? 'hover' : 'hover focus',
                };
            }
        });
    });
});
// Trigger tooltips from other elements
$(document).ready(function() {
    $('[data-custom-tooltip-trigger]').hover(
        function(e) {
            const tooltipElToTrigger = $(e.target).data('custom-tooltip-trigger');
            if (tooltipElToTrigger) {
                $(tooltipElToTrigger).tooltip('toggle');
            }
        },
    );
});

// Toggle password visibility
$(document).on('click', '.show-password', function () {
	const passwordInput = $(this).siblings('input[type="password"], input[type="text"]');
	const eyeIcon = $(this).find('img');
	const isVisible = passwordInput.attr('type') === 'text';

	passwordInput.attr('type', isVisible ? 'password' : 'text');
	eyeIcon.attr({
		src: isVisible
			? '/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/icons/eye-gray.svg'
			: '/config/basketnews.lt/_v2-images/icons/eye-off-gray.svg',
		alt: isVisible
			? "Show password"
			: "Hide password",
		title: isVisible
			? "Show password"
			: "Hide password"
	});
});

// Load Marfeel visitor counts dynamically
function initMarfeelVisitorCounts() {
    // Target only the dedicated visitor count placeholders
    var visitorCountElements = document.querySelectorAll('.marfeel-visitor-count[data-news-id]');
    if (visitorCountElements.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    // Build map of news IDs found on the page to their DOM elements
    var elementMap = {};
    visitorCountElements.forEach(function(el) {
        var newsId = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-news-id'), 10);
        if (newsId) {
            if (!elementMap[newsId]) {
                elementMap[newsId] = [];
            }
            elementMap[newsId].push(el);
        }
    });

    fetch('/index.php?module=news&action=visitorCounts')
        .then(function(response) {
            return response.json();
        })
        .then(function(data) {
            if (data && data.data) {
                var counts = data.data;

                for (var newsId in counts) {
                    if (counts.hasOwnProperty(newsId) && elementMap[newsId]) {
                        var count = counts[newsId];

                        elementMap[newsId].forEach(function(el) {
                            el.textContent = "Currently reading: " + count.toString();

                            var container = el.parentElement;
                            var titleEl = null;

                            while (container && container !== document.body && !titleEl) {
                                titleEl = container.querySelector('.item-post-title, .news-feature__title, .news-tile__title, a.name');
                                if (titleEl) {
                                    break;
                                }

                                container = container.parentElement;
                            }

                            // Prepend fire emoji to the nearest title (only once per title; skip sensitive)
                            if (count >= 500) {
                                if (titleEl && !titleEl.getAttribute('data-fire-prepended') && !titleEl.classList.contains('is-sensitive')) {
                                    titleEl.setAttribute('data-fire-prepended', '1');
                                    titleEl.insertAdjacentText('afterbegin', '🔥 ');
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        })
        .catch(function(error) {
            console.debug('Marfeel visitor count API error:', error);
        });
}

// Load total page view counts for news items
function initNewsViewCounts() {
    var totalViewElements = document.querySelectorAll('.view-counter[data-news-id]:not(.skip-js-view-counter)');
    if (totalViewElements.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    var CACHE_KEY = 'bn-news-view-counts';
    var CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

    function readCache() {
        try {
            var raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
            return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
        } catch (e) {
            return {};
        }
    }

    function writeCache(cache) {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cache));
        } catch (e) {
            // Ignore quota / private-mode errors
        }
    }

    // Build map of news IDs to their DOM elements
    var elementMap = {};
    totalViewElements.forEach(function(el) {
        var newsId = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-news-id'), 10);
        if (!newsId) {
            return;
        }
        if (!elementMap[newsId]) {
            elementMap[newsId] = [];
        }
        elementMap[newsId].push(el);
    });

    var ids = Object.keys(elementMap);
    if (ids.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    function applyCountToElements(newsId, count) {
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(elementMap, newsId)) {
            return;
        }

        elementMap[newsId].forEach(function(el) {
            el.textContent = count.toString();

            var container = el.parentElement;
            var viewCounterSeparator = container.querySelector('.view-counter-separator');

            if (viewCounterSeparator) {
                viewCounterSeparator.classList.remove('d-none');
            }
        });
    }

    var cache = readCache();
    var now = Date.now();
    var idsToFetch = [];

    ids.forEach(function(newsId) {
        var entry = cache[newsId];
        if (entry && entry.ts && (now - entry.ts) < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
            if (entry.count !== null) {
                applyCountToElements(newsId, entry.count);
            }
        } else {
            idsToFetch.push(newsId);
        }
    });

    if (idsToFetch.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    fetch('/news/view-counts.json', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: idsToFetch })
    })
    .then(function(response) {
        return response.json();
    })
    .then(function(payload) {
        if (!payload || !payload.data) {
            return;
        }

        var counts = payload.data;
        var cache = readCache();
        var fetchedAt = Date.now();

        idsToFetch.forEach(function(newsId) {
            if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(counts, newsId)) {
                var count = counts[newsId] || 0;
                cache[newsId] = { count: count, ts: fetchedAt };
                applyCountToElements(newsId, count);
            } else {
                cache[newsId] = { count: null, ts: fetchedAt };
            }
        });

        writeCache(cache);
    })
    .catch(function(error) {
        console.debug('Total views API error:', error);
    });
}

// A news photo can carry a linked game score overlay. Clicking the score opens the game;
// clicking the photo/rest of the card still opens the news (the card is one big <a>).
if (!window.__bnFinalScoreClickBound) {
    window.__bnFinalScoreClickBound = true;
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        var score = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.final-score[data-game-url]') : null;
        if (!score) {
            return;
        }
        var url = score.getAttribute('data-game-url');
        if (!url) {
            return;
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey) {
            window.open(url, '_blank');
        } else {
            window.location.href = url;
        }
    });
}

initMarfeelVisitorCounts();
initNewsViewCounts();
</script>

<script>
    window.BN_LANGUAGE = 'en';
    if (window.jQuery && window.BN_LANGUAGE) {
        var isLangPrefixAllowed = function(url) {
            if (!url) {
                return false;
            }
            var link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = url;

            if (link.host && link.host !== window.location.host) {
                return false;
            }

            var path = link.pathname || '/';
            if (path === '/') {
                return false;
            }

            var basename = path.split('/').pop();
            if (!basename || basename.indexOf('.') === -1 || basename === 'index.php') {
                return true;
            }

            return path.slice(-5) === '.html';
        };

        var appendLangToUrl = function(url, lang) {
            if (!url || url.indexOf('lang=') !== -1) {
                return url;
            }
            return url + (url.indexOf('?') === -1 ? '?' : '&') + 'lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang);
        };

        var isJsonRequestData = function(data, contentType) {
            var ct = (contentType || '').toLowerCase();
            if (ct.indexOf('application/json') !== -1) {
                return true;
            }
            if (typeof data === 'string' && data.length) {
                var first = data.charCodeAt(0);
                return first === 123 || first === 91;
            }
            return false;
        };

        $.ajaxPrefilter(function(options, originalOptions) {
            if (!window.BN_LANGUAGE) {
                return;
            }
            var lang = window.BN_LANGUAGE;

            if (options.url && options.url.indexOf('lang=') !== -1) {
                return;
            }

            if (options.url && !isLangPrefixAllowed(options.url)) {
                return;
            }

            var contentType = options.contentType || (originalOptions && originalOptions.contentType) || '';

            if (originalOptions && originalOptions.data) {
                if (typeof originalOptions.data === 'string') {
                    if (originalOptions.data.indexOf('lang=') !== -1) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (isJsonRequestData(originalOptions.data, contentType)) {
                        options.url = appendLangToUrl(options.url, lang);
                        return;
                    }
                    originalOptions.data += (originalOptions.data ? '&' : '') + 'lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang);
                    options.data = originalOptions.data;
                    return;
                }

                if (typeof originalOptions.data === 'object') {
                    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(originalOptions.data, 'lang')) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (typeof options.data === 'string') {
                        if (isJsonRequestData(options.data, contentType)) {
                            options.url = appendLangToUrl(options.url, lang);
                        } else {
                            options.data += (options.data ? '&' : '') + 'lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang);
                        }
                    } else if (options.url.indexOf('?') === -1) {
                        options.url += '?lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang);
                    } else {
                        options.url += '&lang=' + encodeURIComponent(lang);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }

            options.url = appendLangToUrl(options.url, lang);
        });
    }
</script>
